## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can have a coding task fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 실제 작업을 맡아 처리할 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 폴더의 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀하시면 바로 시작할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 편히 쉬세요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くことを保証する」、UDPは「速さを優先して保証はしない」プロトコルです。

TCPは届いたか確認し、届かなければ再送し、順番も揃えます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、送りっぱなしにします。そのぶん遅延が少なく、オンライン対戦やビデオ通話、DNSのような用途に向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama privada al día con la base y dejar el historial lineal, siempre que nadie más haya usado esos commits. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cómo se desarrolló el trabajo, porque reescribir commits publicados obliga a los demás a arreglar sus copias.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的传输：先握手建立连接，数据有确认、丢了会重传、按顺序交付；UDP 则是不可靠、无连接的：发了就不管，快但不保证到达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpublished branch when you want a clean, linear history before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits that others may have based work on.
```

If you can't reproduce it, say that and why. ⏎  ⏎ Evidence to report: the CI run id or link, the failing job and step names, and the key log lines or test output that show the cause. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not push, do not open a PR, do not merge anything, and do not change the base branch. Do not re-run or cancel CI runs on the remote. A small local fix is not needed for this task; if you find the cause, report it and propose the fix in words, and stop there. Committing an investigation note is not needed either. ⏎  ⏎ Place: the repo at ~/dev/hawdb. Haw
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```sh
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```sh
bun test parse
```

如果想只跑某个 `test(...)` 或 `describe(...)` 名字里匹配的用例，用 `-t`：

```sh
bun test -t "解析空字符串"
```

这三种可以组合使用，比如 `bun test parse -t "空字符串"`。
```

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays the log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done. The board is up with the two nights we have, Night 2 (181k writes/s, p99 4.0 ms) and Night 3 (182k, p99 4.1 ms, no compaction stalls). Each new nightly run will add itself to it.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1782 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nightly runs yet. The next one fills this in.</div>
</main>
<script>
function render() {
  var nights = ducter.get("nights") || [];
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
  list.textContent = "";
  if (!nights.length) {
    empty.hidden = false;
    document.getElementById("latest").textContent = "—";
    document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent = "";
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById("latest").textContent = last.wps + " writes/s";
  document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent = last.night + " · p99 " + last.p99 + (last.stalls === 0 ? " · no compaction stalls" : "");
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
    var row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    var main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = n.night;
    var sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = "p99 " + n.p99 + (n.stalls === 0 ? " · no compaction stalls" : n.stalls != null ? " · " + n.stalls + " compaction stalls" : "");
    main.appendChild(sub);
    var val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = n.wps + "/s";
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","wps":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":null},{"night":"Night 3","wps":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，记一笔就自动按天加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3807 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 · 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0.00</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">按天</h2>
  <div id="days"></div>
  <p class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有花销。点下面「记一笔」开始。</p>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="fd" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="fw" placeholder="午饭"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="fa" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var items = [];
  var picked = null;
  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var esc = function (s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  };
  var money = function (n) { return '¥' + Number(n).toFixed(2); };
  var sumOf = function (list) {
    return list.reduce(function (s, i)  … on (v) {
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  $('days').onclick = function (e) {
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
      items = items.filter(function (i) { return i.id !== id; });
      picked = null;
      save();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
    if (row) {
      var rid = row.getAttribute('data-id');
      picked = picked === rid ? null : rid;
      render();
    }
  };

  $('add').onclick = function () {
    var date = $('fd').value.trim();
    var what = $('fw').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('fa').value);
    if (!date || !what || !(amount > 0)) return;
    items.push({
      id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      date: date,
      what: what,
      amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100
    });
    $('fw').value = '';
    $('fa').value = '';
    save();
  };

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通", open=null;
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
function sel(){document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",b.dataset.c===cat?"true":"false"))}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;sel()});
fu … ";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";
    m.textContent=x.trip||"未命名";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";
    s.textContent=[x.day,x.cat,x.from].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    m.appendChild(s);
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(x.amt||0);
    r.appendChild(m);r.appendChild(v);
    r.onclick=()=>{open=open===i?null:i;draw()};
    list.appendChild(r);
    if(open===i){const d=document.createElement("button");d.className="k-btn";d.textContent="删除这笔";d.onclick=ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();const ys=ducter.get("items")||[];ys.splice(i,1);ducter.set("items",ys);open=null;draw()};list.appendChild(d)}
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const trip=document.getElementById("trip").value.trim();
  const amt=Number(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!trip||!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip,day:document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),cat,amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
sel();draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}
  [also used tools] app

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 那个分支上补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

背景：你上一步已把 TTL 测试改成假时钟。现在请在同一个分支上再补一条测试，覆盖缓存项正好到期的那一刻。

要求：
- 用同一个假时钟，把时间推进到恰好等于过期时刻（不多一毫秒），断言此时该值已经过期（取不到）。
- 再推进一毫秒以内的时间（比过期时刻早一点）时，断言值仍然存在。若现有实现的边界语义不同（例如到期时刻是否算过期），以现有代码的约定为准，并在报告里写明你采用的边界。
- 不要用真实计时（sleep / 真实时间）。
- 不改动已有测试的预期。

完成标准：新测试通过；整个测试套件连跑 20 次全部通过；报告里附上测试输出和新测试的断言内容。

范围之外：不要合并，不要推送，不要开 PR。提交在当前分支上即可。
```
